- **premortem**: Imagines the project has failed three months from now and works backward to find the likely causes, writing reviews/premortem.md. Use proactively before a launch, release, store submission, big refactor or migration. ([ISenseHostility/jarno-plugins](https://github.com/ISenseHostility/jarno-plugins/blob/HEAD/premortem/agents/premortem.md))
- **premortem**: Pre-execution plan attacker. Use PROACTIVELY between planning and execution on any T3 task — after a worker (or the main agent) has produced its AUQ, step plan, and gates, but before substantive work begins. Given the brief and the plan artifacts, it assumes the deliverable already failed, writes the most plausible postmortems, and converts them into a small set of concrete plan amendments. Cheap and fast by design; minutes, not hours. Never executes the plan. Not for reviewing finished work — that is mega-opus-redteam's job. ([weisberg/dotclaude](https://github.com/weisberg/dotclaude/blob/HEAD/agents/premortem.md))
- **premortem-agent**: Imagine the incident a shipped work unit would cause, working backward to find the missing guard ([rakovi4/continue-example](https://github.com/rakovi4/continue-example/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/premortem-agent.md) · ★2)
- **premortem-agent**: Attacks one idea in execution, in a fresh context that did not generate it — the baselines it must beat and whether they run, the field's own metric, what the evaluation plan depends on, and a three-state verdict on whether three months of this would produce a number. Judges execution, never novelty. Dispatched by the premortem skill, up to five at a time. ([rbh227/research-bearings](https://github.com/rbh227/research-bearings/blob/HEAD/agents/premortem-agent.md) · ★1)

- **prentun**: QR-merkin og miðaprentunin — Brother PT-P750W, raðnúmerakerfið, QR-skanninn og allar prentleiðirnar. Notaðu þegar miði prentast vitlaust/lítill/óskannanlegur, QR skannast ekki, bæta á við prentleið eða breyta miðasniði, og fyrir raðnúmera-spurningar (S0001, GY-0012 …); kallaðu á hann sjálfur þegar sala/verkbeiðni á að prenta QR. Rödd í Jarvis: 🏷️ DeVito. Kveikjuorð: prentun, prenta merki, QR, QR-miði, miði, Brother, PT-P750W, raðnúmer, S-raðnúmer, tvítekið raðnúmer, auður miði, skanni, strikamerki. - **preop-judge**: Pre-op plan judge — the FIRST gate of the /scientific-method harness, pinned to Fable. Reviews an empirical test PLAN before it runs — does the method actually DISCRIMINATE H1 from H0 and the named confounds, are the controls real, is the decision rule pre-committed and falsifiable — and returns APPROVED / APPROVED-WITH-REVISIONS / REJECTED. Judges the METHOD from the pasted packet, not the repo. No approval, no run. ([Infernal-Crack-LED/nikke-sim](https://github.com/Infernal-Crack-LED/nikke-sim/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/preop-judge.md))
- **prep**: Pre-meeting prep assistant for LetPeopleAlign. Before the next meeting of a series, reads the stored notes, the action ledger, and any drift observations, and produces a short alignment-framed prep sheet — open actions to chase (with age), topics left undecided, and position shifts worth confirming together. Never an ambush. Use right before the next meeting of a series. ([LetPeopleWork/LetPeopleAlign](https://github.com/LetPeopleWork/LetPeopleAlign/blob/HEAD/agents/prep.md))
- **prep**: 1:1 prep assistant. Before a 1:1, reads the whole per-person history (profile, past sessions, growth plan, patterns) and produces a one-page prep — open commitments from both sides, quiet/stalled threads, energy trend, a pattern flag or two, and 2-3 suggested questions grounded in the conversation-practices library and THIS person. Use right before a 1:1. ([LetPeopleWork/LetPeopleGrow](https://github.com/LetPeopleWork/LetPeopleGrow/blob/HEAD/agents/prep.md))
- **prep**: Prep cook. Runs one recipe from work/recipes/ exactly as written (setup commands, builds, checks, runs of report tools, log summaries, mechanical edits) and reports in a few lines. For mechanical, self-checking steps; never for design or judgment calls. ([Yosh145/brigade](https://github.com/Yosh145/brigade/blob/HEAD/agents/prep.md))
- **prep-agent**: **Purpose:** Fast setup for issue implementation - create branch, fetch issue. ([hamiltonroad/checked-out](https://github.com/hamiltonroad/checked-out/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/prep-agent.md))
- **prep-auditor**: Spawned by /prep Step 6c, after drift-detector returns. Adversarially audits a phase document for unstated assumptions, failure modes, UX gaps, blast radius, security gaps, and implementation ambiguity, and returns a one-line-per-finding punch list (HIGH/MED/LOW with an action tag). Takes the orchestrator's ground-truth findings and the drift report as input so it doesn't repeat them. Read-only. ([sababado/fullstack-template-rep](https://github.com/sababado/fullstack-template-rep/blob/HEAD/template/.claude/agents/prep-auditor.md))
- **prep-cook**: Fast, cheap helper for simple searches, summaries and repetitive mechanical edits. Use to locate code, list or count things, summarize a file, or apply one small identical edit in several places. Never for ambiguous, risky or design work. ([Velavalapallisairam/Auto-Router](https://github.com/Velavalapallisairam/Auto-Router/blob/HEAD/agents/prep-cook.md) · ★1)
- **prep-cook**: Cheap, fast grunt work - codebase search, renames, boilerplate, bulk mechanical edits, file conversion/transcription, log reading. Use via the router skill. ([chaelri/chaelri.github.io](https://github.com/chaelri/chaelri.github.io/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/prep-cook.md))
- **prep-kit**: **Purpose:** Fast setup for issue implementation - create branch, fetch issue, generate context hints. ([hamiltonroad/checked-out](https://github.com/hamiltonroad/checked-out/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/prep-kit.md))
- **prep-master**: Runs the job-prep queue for /ohmyjob:prep-jobs. Screens every "Found" job with one screen-worker per job and builds a resume for every "Matched" job with one resume-worker per job, as a queue (a new worker starts as soon as one finishes); scripts do all the bookkeeping. - **preparador-imagenes**: Prepara e integra las imágenes de "Armado en México" — armas, accesorios, municiones, campos de tiro, cursos y portadas de categoría.
